129	 *   涂层（paintCoating 1/2 → fullbright/invisible 位）系统未建，另行登记。
130	 */
131	export function applyPaintTint(
132	  r: number, g: number, b: number, paint: number,
133	): readonly [number, number, number] {
134	  if (paint <= 0) return [r, g, b];
135	  if (paint === 26) return [r, g, b];                   // 白漆：乘 255 ≡ 原色
136	  if (paint === 30) return [255 - r, 255 - g, 255 - b]; // 负相：反转
137	  const [pr, pg, pb] = paintColor(paint);
138	  // 逐通道乘色（除 26/30 外全部走此式：1-24 浅/深层 + 25/27/28/29 黑灰棕暗影）
139	  return [(r * pr) >> 8, (g * pg) >> 8, (b * pb) >> 8];
140	}

146	 */
147	export function mapPaintColor(
148	  isWall: boolean, rgb: readonly [number, number, number], colorType: number,
149	): readonly [number, number, number] {
150	  const color = paintColor(colorType); // :1814
151	  let num = rgb[0] / 255, num2 = rgb[1] / 255, num3 = rgb[2] / 255; // :1815-1817
152	  if (num2 > num) { const t = num; num = num2; num2 = t; } // :1818-1823
153	  if (num3 > num) { const t = num; num = num3; num3 = t; } // :1824-1829
154	  let or_ = rgb[0], og = rgb[1], ob = rgb[2];
155	  switch (colorType) {
156	    case 29: { // ShadowPaint :1832-1839
157	      const num7 = num3 * 0.3;
158	      or_ = (color[0] * num7) | 0; og = (color[1] * num7) | 0; ob = (color[2] * num7) | 0;
159	      break;
160	    }
161	    case 30: // NegativePaint :1840-1853
162	      if (isWall) {
163	        or_ = ((255 - rgb[0]) * 0.5) | 0; og = ((255 - rgb[1]) * 0.5) | 0; ob = ((255 - rgb[2]) * 0.5) | 0;
164	      } else {
165	        or_ = 255 - rgb[0]; og = 255 - rgb[1]; ob = 255 - rgb[2];
166	      }
167	      break;
168	    default: { // 普通漆/深层/黑白灰棕 :1854-1861——按最大通道缩放油漆色
169	      const num6 = num;
170	      or_ = (color[0] * num6) | 0; og = (color[1] * num6) | 0; ob = (color[2] * num6) | 0;
171	      break;
172	    }
173	  }
174	  return [or_, og, ob];
175	}

206	export function paintTile(
207	  st: PaintableStore, x: number, y: number, color: number, dust: PaintDustHook | null = null,

				Utils.DrawRectangle(spriteBatch, start, end, Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Color.LightSeaGreen, Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Color.LightSeaGreen, 2f);
			}
		}
		TimeLogger.DrawWireTiles.AddTime(fromTimestamp);
	}

	public static int ConvertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex(int paintIndexOnTile, bool isUsedForPaintingGrass, bool useWallShaderHacks)
	{
		if (paintIndexOnTile == 31)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		if (paintIndexOnTile == 30 && useWallShaderHacks)
		{
			return 43;
		}
		if (paintIndexOnTile >= 28)
		{
			return paintIndexOnTile + 12;
		}
		if (isUsedForPaintingGrass && paintIndexOnTile >= 1 && paintIndexOnTile <= 12)
		{
			return paintIndexOnTile + 27;
		}
		return paintIndexOnTile;
	}

	public static void ResetWindCounter(bool resetExtreme = false)
	{
		FastRandom fastRandom = FastRandom.CreateWithRandomSeed();
		windCounter = fastRandom.Next(900, 2701);
		if (resetExtreme)
		{
			extremeWindCounter = fastRandom.Next(10, 31);
		}
	}

But hmm, actually, wait. Let me reconsider the third swap. After swap1: (M, m, b) where M=max(r,g) permuted positions. After swap2 on xz: (max(M,b), min(M,b)) in x,z; y still m. After swap3 on yz: y' = max(m, min(M,b)), z' = min(m, min(M,b)).

If the pass needed the median, it would use y' = max(m, min(M,b)). The disasm computes `min r0.z, r2.y, r1.y` (that's z') but never max(r1.y, r2.y). So passes genuinely use r2.y = min(M,b) — the semi-sorted middle slot.

```
Final: out.r = hi × v0.r; out.g = (hi+lo)/2 × v0.g; out.b = lo × v0.z; out.a = a × v0.a.

Note the v0 handling: v0 = vertex color (usually white = 1). The (hi+lo)×0.5: they multiply (hi+lo) by v0.y AND by 0.5. So out.g = (hi+lo) × v0.g / 2. With v0 = white, = (hi+lo)/2.

out = (hi, (hi+lo)/2, lo).

Pass4 (paint 3 YELLOW): out = (hi, mid*, lo).

mul r0.xyzw, r0.xyzw, r1.xyzw

out = ((hi+lo)/2 × v0.x, hi × v0.y, lo × v0.z). With white v0: ((hi+lo)/2, hi, lo). LIME = (127,255,0) = (0.5, 1, 0) ✓ consistent with lerp! interesting: lime matched lerp.

So shallow paints:
- 1 RED: (hi, lo, lo) = lerp ✓
- 2 ORANGE: (hi, (hi+lo)/2, lo) = lerp ✓
- 3 YELLOW: (hi, mid*, lo) ≠ lerp (out.g = mid* instead of hi)
- 4 LIME: ((hi+lo)/2, hi, lo) = lerp ✓
- 5 GREEN: (lo, hi, lo) ✓
- 6 TEAL: (lo, hi, (hi+lo)/2) ✓
- 7 CYAN: (lo, hi, mid*) ≠ lerp (out.b = mid*)
- 8 SKY_BLUE: (lo, (hi+lo)/2, hi) ✓
- 9 BLUE: (lo, lo, hi) ✓
- 10 PURPLE: ((hi+lo)/2, lo, hi) ✓
- 11 VIOLET: (hi, lo, mid*) ≠ lerp (out.b = mid*)
- 12 PINK: (hi, lo, (hi+lo)/2) ✓

So the anomalies: YELLOW (g), CYAN (b), VIOLET (b) — the channels that would equal hi but are the "second" occurrence of hi in the paint color. Interesting: it's as if the HLSL assigns, per paint, from a partially-sorted triple (hi, mid*, lo) where the paint color's channels map: 1st full-bright channel → hi; a second full-bright channel → mid* (the partially sorted middle); 0.5 → average; 0 → lo.

// s.x = hi, s.y = mid, s.z = lo
```

and passes like YELLOW used `(s.x, s.x, s.z)`. If so, the compiled result for out.g would be max(m, min(M,b)) — needing an extra max instruction which is NOT in the disasm. The disasm only has `min r0.z, r2.y, r1.y` (computing lo) and r2.y used directly. So the HLSL must NOT compute the true median — the shader source itself uses min(max(r,g), b) — OR the compiler proved the extra max unnecessary because... it can't.

OK whatever — bytecode truth. But wait, I want to double-check the "cmp r2.y, r1.w, r1.z, r1" reading once more. Registers: `r1.w` (cond), `r1.z` (b), `r1` — the disassembler omits `.x` swizzle. In line 34 `cmp r2.x, r1.w, r1, r1.z` the middle source `r1` should be r1.x (= M) for max-of-3 to make sense. ✓. So line 35 `cmp r2.y, r1.w, r1.z, r1`: last source r1 = r1.x = M. So r2.y = (M-b >= 0) ? b : M = min(M, b). Confirmed.

Let me also double check Pass2's `min r0.yz, r2.y, r1.y`: r2.y = min(M,b), r1.y = min(r,g). min of those = min3 ✓.

And in Pass4: out.g = r2.y = min(M,b) = mid*. OK.

Now — is mid* ever observable vs true median? mid* = min(max(r,g), b); true median = max(min(r,g), min(max(r,g), b)) = max(m, mid*). mid* ≠ median iff m > mid*, i.e., m > min(M,b) iff (m > b is false? let's see) m > min(M,b) ⟺ b < m (since m <= M always, min(M,b) < m only when b < m). So mid* ≠ median exactly when b < min(r,g) — i.e., blue is the strict minimum channel. In that case mid* = b = lo.

So for yellow paint on a pixel with b = strict min (very common — e.g. brownish/tan pixels (0.8, 0.6, 0.4)): out = (hi, b, lo) = (0.8, 0.4, 0.4). Under lerp it'd be (0.8, 0.8, 0.4). Big visual difference! And this is bytecode truth. 

Hmm hold on, wait. Let me reconsider whether the sort might be different — maybe the compiler chose to sort (g,b) first and compare with r, and I got the pair wrong. Let me recheck: `max r1.x, r0, r0.y` — sources r0 and r0.y. If `r0` (no suffix) = r0.x, then r1.x = max(r0.x, r0.y) = max(r, g) ✓. `min r1.y, r0.y, r0` = min(g, r) ✓. Then compare with r0.z (b). So sort pair = (r, g), compare vs b. Yes: mid* = min(max(r,g), b).

So in JS: 
```js
const hi = Math.max(r, g, b);
const lo = Math.min(r, g, b);
const midStar = Math.min(Math.max(r, g), b);
```

Summary of deep formula vs shallow: shallow lerp channels: 1 → hi, 0.5 → (hi+lo)/2, 0 → lo. Deep: 1 → hi, 0.5 → (hi + 0.4·lo)/2, 0 → 0.4·lo. So deep = same structure with lo replaced by 0.4·lo in the non-hi slots, and mid* replaces "second hi slot" like shallow. I.e., deep paints darken the low end by ×0.4.

So deep variant: out.c = hi if paint.c==max; = mid* if paint.c is the second max channel (in the same anomalous way); = (hi + 0.4·lo)/2 if paint.c == 0.5-ish; = 0.4·lo if paint.c == 0.

Let me double-check deep anomaly channels: DEEP_YELLOW out.g = mid* (from `mov r0.xy, r2`) ✓ same as shallow YELLOW. DEEP_CYAN out.z = mid* ✓. DEEP_VIOLET out.z = mid* ✓.

So deep/shallow differ exactly: lo → 0.4·lo in low slots, and (hi+lo)/2 → (hi+0.4·lo)/2.

Whoa — WHITE paint is NOT identity! Let me sanity check: for a white pixel (1,1,1): hi=lo=1 → (0.7+0.3)×(2−1) = 1×1 = 1 ✓ stays white. For black (0,0,0): 0×2 = 0 ✓. For gray 0.5: (0.35+0.15)×(2−0.5) = 0.5×1.5 = 0.75. So gray 0.5 → 0.75 — brighter! White paint brightens mid tones. Interesting — that's "white paint makes things whiter". For pixel (1, 0, 0): hi=1, lo=0 → 0.7 × 1.5 = 1.05 → clamps to 1 at output (sat). OK.

max r2.w, r0, r0.y → max(r, g)

mul r3.y, r3, v0.y → r3.y = hi × v0.g

```
out.r = v0.r × r3.x = v0.r × hi; out.g = 0.7 × hi × v0.g; out.b = 0.49 × hi × v0.b; out.a = v0.a × a.

BROWN: out = (hi, 0.7·hi, 0.49·hi). Note: uses only hi, not lo! Brown paint color (255,178,125): 178/255 = 0.698 ✓, 125/255 = 0.49 ✓. So brown = hue-ize by scaling hi with paint channel ratios.

Pass43 (index 42, paint 30 NEGATIVE, no wall hacks, c0 = 1.0):
```
max r1.w, r0, r0.y → max(r,g)
max r2.w, r1.w, r0.z → hi
add r1.x, -r0, c0 → 1 - r
cmp r0.x, -r2.w, r0, r1 → r0.x = (-hi >= 0) ? r : (1-r)
```
Since hi >= 0 always, -hi <= 0; -hi >= 0 only if hi == 0. So out.c = (hi == 0) ? c : (1 - c). I.e., if the pixel is pure black (all channels 0), keep it; else invert. Makes sense: don't invert fully-black pixels (transparent black outlines stay black).

Then `mul r0, r0, v0`.

NEGATIVE: out.c = (hi == 0) ? c : 255 - c. In 0..1 terms: pixel (0,0,0) → stays (0,0,0). Otherwise invert per channel.

max r0.x, r1, c0.z → max(that, 0)
```
per channel: out.c = max( (hi == 0) ? 2c : 1.5 − 2c , 0 ).

Wall negative: if black pixel: 2c (double brightness); else 1.5 − 2c clamped at 0 (a steeper inversion that darkens and clamps). Interesting.

Standard hue computation (HLSL):
```
float maxc = max(r, max(g,b));
float minc = min(r, min(g,b));
float delta = maxc - minc;
float3 br = (maxc - rgb) / delta;  // or rgb - minc...
// hue = ...
```

Common formula (like the one in the shader "hue = fmod(6 * something, 6)"):

Terraria's known special-group shader computes hue in [0,1) as:
```
float hue;
if (r == maxc) hue = (g - b) / delta;        // between yellow & magenta
else if (g == maxc) hue = 2 + (b - r) / delta;
else hue = 4 + (r - g) / delta;
hue /= 6; if (hue < 0) hue += 1;
```

The c6 constant (6, 2, 4, 1/6) strongly suggests exactly this: sector bases 0 (implicit), 2, 4, and the /6, with frc and conditional add.

148	        const infoOff = u(8 + 16);           // ConstantInfo@16
149	        const syms = [];
150	        for (let i = 0; i < nConst; i++) {
151	          const e = p + 8 + infoOff + i * 20;            // CINFO 20B：name u32 + u16×4 + u32×2
152	          const nameOff = b.getUint32(e, true);
153	          const regSet = b.getUint16(e + 4, true);
154	          const regIndex = b.getUint16(e + 6, true);
155	          const regCount = b.getUint16(e + 8, true);
156	          const name = blob.toString('latin1', p + 8 + nameOff).split('\0')[0];
157	          syms.push({ name, regSet, regIndex, regCount });
158	        }
159	        return syms;
160	      }
161	      p += size * 4;
162	    } else {
163	      p += 4;
164	    }
165	  }
166	  return [];
167	}
168	
169	// ---------- Preshader（PRES 注释段：CLIT 字面量 + PRSI 输出映射 + FXLC 指令 VM）----------
170	// 结构（mojoshader.c parse_preshader :2446-2800）：子注释 token（size=数据 token 数）
171	// 携带 CTAB/PRSI/CLIT/FXLC 四字符块。
172	const PSOP = {
173	  0x1000: 'mov', 0x1010: 'neg', 0x1030: 'rcp', 0x1040: 'frc', 0x1050: 'exp', 0x1060: 'log',
174	  0x1070: 'rsq', 0x1080: 'sin', 0x1090: 'cos', 0x10A0: 'asin', 0x10B0: 'acos', 0x10C0: 'atan',
175	  0x2000: 'min', 0x2010: 'max', 0x2020: 'lt', 0x2030: 'ge', 0x2040: 'add', 0x2050: 'mul',
176	  0x2060: 'atan2', 0x2080: 'div', 0x3000: 'cmp', 0x3010: 'movc', 0x5000: 'dot', 0x5020: 'noise',
177	  0xA000: 'min_s', 0xA010: 'max_s', 0xA020: 'lt_s', 0xA030: 'ge_s', 0xA040: 'add_s',
178	  0xA050: 'mul_s', 0xA060: 'atan2_s', 0xA080: 'div_s', 0xD000: 'dot_s', 0xD020: 'noise_s',
179	};
180	function parsePreshader(blob, presAt, presSizeTok) {
181	  const b = new DataView(blob.buffer, blob.byteOffset, blob.byteLength);
182	  const tu = (o) => b.getUint32(presAt + o, true);
183	  const lines = [];
184	  let p = 8;                              // 跳过 PRES 四字符 + version token（0x46580201）
185	  const end = 4 + presSizeTok * 4 - 4;
186	  const blocks = {};
187	  while (p + 4 <= end) {
188	    const t = tu(p);
189	    if ((t & 0xFFFF) !== 0xFFFE) break;   // 0xFFFF 子 EOS
190	    const size = (t >>> 16) & 0x7FFF;
191	    const id = blob.toString('latin1', presAt + p + 4, presAt + p + 8);
192	    if (size > 0) blocks[id] = { at: presAt + p + 4, size: size - 1 };   // 四字符在 comment token+4；内容 token=size−1
193	    p += 4 + size * 4;
194	  }
195	  if (!blocks.CLIT || !blocks.FXLC) return lines;
196	  const bu = (blk) => (o) => b.getUint32(blk.at + o, true);
197	  // CLIT：[fourcc][count][count 个 double]
198	  const clit = blocks.CLIT, cu = bu(clit);
199	  const litCount = cu(4);
200	  const lits = [];
201	  for (let i = 0; i < litCount; i++) lits.push(b.getFloat64(clit.at + 8 + i * 8, true));
202	  // preshader CTAB：符号→寄存器（INPUT item = regIndex*4+comp）
203	  let syms = [];
204	  if (blocks.CTAB) {
205	    const cb = blocks.CTAB, cbu = bu(cb);
206	    const nConst = cbu(4 + 12), infoOff = cbu(4 + 16);
207	    for (let i = 0; i < nConst; i++) {
208	      const e = 4 + infoOff + i * 20;
209	      const nameOff = cbu(e);
210	      const regIndex = b.getUint16(cb.at + e + 6, true);
211	      const regCount = b.getUint16(cb.at + e + 8, true);
212	      syms.push({
213	        name: blob.toString('latin1', cb.at + 4 + nameOff).split('\0')[0],
214	        regIndex, regCount,
215	      });
216	    }
217	  }
218	  const symOf = (item) => {
219	    for (const s of syms) {
220	      const base = s.regIndex * 4, cnt = s.regCount * 4;
221	      if (base <= item && base + cnt > item) return `${s.name}${s.regCount > 1 ? `[${Math.floor((item - base) / 4)}]` : '.' + 'xyzw'[item % 4]}`;
222	    }
223	    return `in${item}`;
224	  };
225	  // PRSI：tokens[7]=output_map_count，之后 (base,count) 对
226	  let outMap = [];
227	  if (blocks.PRSI) {
228	    const pb = blocks.PRSI, pu = bu(pb);
229	    const n = pu(7 * 4);
230	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) outMap.push([pu(8 * 4 + i * 8), pu(8 * 4 + i * 8 + 4)]);
231	  }
232	  const outOf = (item) => {
233	    for (const [base, cnt] of outMap) {
234	      if (base * 4 <= item && (base + cnt) * 4 > item) return `c${base + Math.floor((item - base * 4) / 4)}`;
235	    }
236	    return `out${item}`;
237	  };
238	  // FXLC：[fourcc][opcode_count] + 每指令（opcodetok, operandcount-1, 每 operand 3 token）
239	  const fx = blocks.FXLC, fu = bu(fx);
240	  let nInst = fu(4);
241	  let q = 8;
242	  while (nInst-- > 0 && q + 8 <= fx.size * 4) {
243	    const opTok = fu(q);
244	    const op = PSOP[(opTok >> 16) & 0xFFFF] ?? `psop0x${((opTok >> 16) & 0xFFFF).toString(16)}`;
245	    const elems = opTok & 0xFF;
246	    const nOps = fu(q + 4) + 1;
247	    q += 8;
248	    const parts = [];
249	    for (let i = 0; i < nOps; i++) {
250	      const numArrays = fu(q), type = fu(q + 4), item = fu(q + 8);
251	      q += 12 + numArrays * 8;
252	      const label = type === 1 ? `lit${item}(${lits[item]})`
253	        : type === 2 ? symOf(item)
254	          : type === 4 ? outOf(item)
255	            : type === 7 ? `t${item}` : `?${item}`;
256	      parts.push((i === 0 ? '' : ', ') + label);
257	    }
258	    lines.push(`    ps.${op}${elems ? `[${elems}]` : ''} ${parts.join('')}`);
259	  }
260	  if (lits.length) lines.push(`    // ps.literals: [${lits.map((x) => +x.toFixed(6)).join(', ')}]`);
261	  return lines;
262	}
263	
264	// ---------- SM2 反汇编 ----------
265	const REGT = ['r', 'v', 'c', 't', 'rast', 'attr', 'o', 'oC', 'oDepth', '?', 's', 'c', 'c', 'c', 'cB', 'aL', 'f16'];  // mojoshader_internal.h RegisterType：10=SAMPLER
266	function regName(tok) {
267	  const type = ((tok >> 8) & 0x18) | ((tok >> 28) & 7);
268	  const num = tok & 0x7FF;
269	  const baseName = REGT[type] ?? `reg${type}`;
270	  if (type === 7) return `oC${num}`;
271	  if (type === 8) return 'oDepth';
272	  return `${baseName}${num}`;
273	}
274	function dstStr(tok) {
275	  const wm = (tok >> 16) & 0xF;
276	  const sat = ((tok >> 20) & 0xF) === 2 ? '_sat' : '';
277	  const mask = '.' + ['x', 'y', 'z', 'w'].filter((_, i) => wm & (1 << i)).join('');  // D3DSP_WRITEMASK_0=1<<16(.x)…_3=1<<19(.w)
278	  return regName(tok) + mask + sat;
279	}
280	const SWZ = ['x', 'y', 'z', 'w'];
281	function srcStr(tok) {
282	  const sw = (tok >> 16) & 0xFF;
283	  const s = [sw & 3, (sw >> 2) & 3, (sw >> 4) & 3, (sw >> 6) & 3];
284	  const sm = (tok >> 24) & 0xF;
285	  const mod = sm === 1 ? '-' : sm === 11 ? 'abs-' : sm === 0 ? '' : `m${sm}`;
286	  const uniq = s.every((c) => c === s[0]);
287	  return mod + regName(tok) + (uniq ? (s[0] === 0 ? '' : `.${SWZ[s[0]]}`) : `.${s.map((c) => SWZ[c]).join('')}`);
288	}
289	// opcode 表（d3d9types.h）：opcode → [name, operand token 数]
290	const OP = {
291	  0: ['nop', 0], 1: ['mov', 2], 2: ['add', 3], 3: ['sub', 3], 4: ['mad', 4], 5: ['mul', 3],
292	  6: ['rcp', 2], 7: ['rsq', 2], 8: ['dp3', 3], 9: ['dp4', 3], 10: ['min', 3], 11: ['max', 3],
293	  12: ['slt', 3], 13: ['sge', 3], 14: ['exp', 2], 15: ['log', 2], 18: ['lrp', 4], 19: ['frc', 2],
294	  31: ['dcl', 2], 32: ['pow', 3], 33: ['crs', 3], 35: ['abs', 2], 36: ['nrm', 2],
295	  37: ['sincos', 4], 40: ['if', 1], 41: ['ifc', 3], 42: ['break', 0], 43: ['breakc', 3],
296	  45: ['defb', 2], 46: ['defi', 5], 64: ['texcrd', 2], 65: ['texkill', 1], 66: ['texld', 3],   // ps_2_0 texld = opcode 0x42（实证 obj4）
297	  81: ['def', 5], 88: ['cmp', 4], 90: ['dp2add', 4], 93: ['texldd', 5], 95: ['texldl', 3],
298	  0xFFFD: ['phase', 0], 0xFFFE: ['comment', 0], 0xFFFF: ['end', 0],
299	};
300	function disasm(blob, syms) {
301	  const b = new DataView(blob.buffer, blob.byteOffset, blob.byteLength);
302	  const lines = [];
303	  const regSym = new Map();   // 'cN' → 参数名
304	  for (const s of syms) if (s.regSet === 0 || s.regSet === 2) {
305	    for (let r = s.regIndex; r < s.regIndex + s.regCount; r++) regSym.set(`c${r}`, s.name + (s.regCount > 1 ? `[${r - s.regIndex}]` : ''));
306	  }
307	  let p = 0;
308	  const verTok = b.getUint32(p, true); p += 4;
309	  const major = (verTok >> 8) & 0xFF, minor = verTok & 0xFF;
310	  lines.push(`    // ${((verTok >> 16) & 0xFFFF) === 0xFFFF ? 'ps' : 'vs'}_${major}_${minor}`);
311	  let maxP = blob.length;
312	  let presAt = -1, presSize = 0;
313	  while (p + 4 <= maxP) {
314	    const t = b.getUint32(p, true); p += 4;
315	    const op = t & 0xFFFF;
316	    if (op === 0xFFFE) {          // 注释段：size 字段=数据 DWORD 数（不含自身）
317	      if (p + 4 <= maxP && blob.toString('latin1', p, p + 4) === 'PRES') { presAt = p; presSize = (t >>> 16) & 0x7FFF; }
318	      p += ((t >>> 16) & 0x7FFF) * 4;
319	      continue;
320	    }
321	    if (op === 0xFFFF) { lines.push('    end'); break; }
322	    const e = OP[op];
323	    if (!e) { lines.push(`    ??? op=${op} raw=0x${t.toString(16)} @${p - 4}`); break; }
324	    const [name, nOps] = e;
325	    if (name === 'comment') { continue; }
326	    const ops = [];
327	    for (let i = 0; i < nOps; i++) { ops.push(b.getUint32(p, true)); p += 4; }
328	    if (name === 'dcl') {
329	      const info = ops[0];
330	      const itype = (info >> 28) & 7 | ((info >> 8) & 0x18);
331	      const texType = info & 0xFFFF;
332	      const tt = texType === 2 ? '2d' : texType === 3 ? 'cube' : texType === 4 ? 'volume' : texType === 1 ? '1d' : '';
333	      if (itype === 9) lines.push(`    dcl_${tt} ${regName(ops[1])}`);
334	      else if (itype === 1) lines.push(`    dcl v${ops[1] & 0x7FF}`);
335	      else if (itype === 3) lines.push(`    dcl t${ops[1] & 0x7FF}`);
336	      else lines.push(`    dcl_${itype} ${ops[1].toString(16)}`);
337	    } else if (name === 'def') {
338	      const f = [];
339	      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) f.push(b.getFloat32(p - 16 + i * 4, true));
340	      lines.push(`    def ${regName(ops[0])}, ${f.map((x) => x.toFixed(6)).join(', ')}`);
341	    } else if (name === 'defb') {
342	      lines.push(`    defb ${regName(ops[0])}, ${ops[1]}`);
343	    } else if (name === 'texld') {
344	      lines.push(`    texld ${dstStr(ops[0])}, ${srcStr(ops[1])}, ${regName(ops[2])}`);
345	    } else {
346	      const ctrl = op === 0x40 ? '' : '';
347	      const parts = ops.map((o, i) => (i === 0 && name !== 'texkill' ? dstStr(o) : srcStr(o)));
348	      lines.push(`    ${name}${ctrl} ${parts.join(', ')}`.trimEnd());
349	    }
350	  }
351	  // cN → 参数名注解
352	  const named = [...regSym.entries()].sort((a, z) => parseInt(a[0].slice(1)) - parseInt(z[0].slice(1)));
353	  if (named.length) lines.push(`    // symbols: ${named.map(([r, n]) => `${r}=${n}`).join(' ')}`);
354	  if (presAt >= 0) lines.push(...parsePreshader(blob, presAt, presSize));
355	  return lines.join('\n');
356	}
357	
358	// ---------- JSON 导出（游戏侧 SM2 解释器消费：字节码级 1:1）----------
359	const jsonIdx = process.argv.indexOf('--json');
360	if (jsonIdx >= 0) {
361	  const passesJson = [];
362	  const seenPass = new Set();
363	  for (const t of techs) {
364	    for (const pass of t.passes) {
365	      if (!pass.name || seenPass.has(pass.name)) continue;   // 重名 pass（ArmorPlaid×2）取首
366	      seenPass.add(pass.name);
367	      for (const s of pass.states) {
368	        const oi = s.value.objects?.[0];
369	        if (oi === undefined || (objType[oi] !== 15 && objType[oi] !== 16)) continue;
370	        const blob = blobs.get(oi);
371	        if (!blob) continue;
372	        // 抽取：指令 token 序列（到 end）+ CTAB 符号 + preshader（块原始 token）
373	        const b = new DataView(blob.buffer, blob.byteOffset, blob.byteLength);
374	        let p = 0;
375	        const verTok = b.getUint32(p, true); p += 4;
376	        if (((verTok >> 16) & 0xFFFF) !== 0xFFFF) continue;   // 仅像素着色器
377	        const tokens = [];
378	        let presTokens = null;
379	        while (p + 4 <= blob.length) {
380	          const tok = b.getUint32(p, true); p += 4;
381	          const op = tok & 0xFFFF;
382	          if (op === 0xFFFE) {
383	            const size = (tok >>> 16) & 0x7FFF;
384	            if (p + 4 <= blob.length && blob.toString('latin1', p, p + 4) === 'PRES') {
385	              presTokens = [];
386	              for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) { presTokens.push(b.getUint32(p + i * 4, true)); }
387	            }
388	            p += size * 4;
389	            continue;
390	          }
391	          tokens.push(tok);
392	          if (op === 0xFFFF) break;
393	        }
394	        // preshader 预解码：presTokens 重建字节缓冲后按字节解析（块布局同 parsePreshader）
395	        let pres = null;
396	        if (presTokens) {
397	          pres = { lits: [], syms: [], outMap: [], insts: [] };
398	          const pb = Buffer.alloc(presTokens.length * 4);
399	          presTokens.forEach((tk, i) => pb.writeUInt32LE(tk >>> 0, i * 4));
400	          const pu32 = (o) => pb.readUInt32LE(o);
401	          let q = 8;                       // PRES 四字符 + version token
402	          while (q + 8 <= pb.length) {
403	            const ct = pu32(q);
404	            if ((ct & 0xFFFF) !== 0xFFFE) break;
405	            const csize = (ct >>> 16) & 0x7FFF;
406	            const id = pb.toString('latin1', q + 4, q + 8);
407	            const ds = q + 8;              // 块内容起点（fourcc 后）
408	            if (id === 'CLIT') {          // 内容=[count][doubles]
409	              const n = pu32(ds);
410	              for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) pres.lits.push(pb.readDoubleLE(ds + 4 + i * 8));
411	            } else if (id === 'PRSI') {   // mojo tokens[7] 以 fourcc 为 [0] → 内容+24
412	              const n = pu32(ds + 24);
413	              for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) pres.outMap.push([pu32(ds + 28 + i * 8), pu32(ds + 32 + i * 8)]);
414	            } else if (id === 'CTAB') {   // 内容起点即 Size 字段（偏移基准）
415	              const st = ds;
416	              const nConst = pu32(st + 12), infoOff = pu32(st + 16);
417	              for (let i = 0; i < nConst; i++) {
418	                const e = st + infoOff + i * 20;          // CINFO 20B
419	                const nameOff = pu32(e);
420	                const regIndex = pb.readUInt16LE(e + 6);
421	                const regCount = pb.readUInt16LE(e + 8);
422	                let nm = '';
423	                for (let b = st + nameOff; b < pb.length && pb[b] !== 0; b++) nm += String.fromCharCode(pb[b]);
424	                pres.syms.push({ name: nm, regIndex, regCount });
425	              }
426	            } else if (id === 'FXLC') {   // 内容=[opcode_count][instrs]
427	              const nInst = pu32(ds);
428	              let r = ds + 4;
429	              for (let i = 0; i < nInst; i++) {
430	                const opTok = pu32(r);
431	                const nOps = pu32(r + 4) + 1;
432	                r += 8;
433	                const ops = [];
434	                for (let j = 0; j < nOps; j++) {
435	                  ops.push([pu32(r), pu32(r + 4), pu32(r + 8)]);
436	                  r += 12 + pu32(r) * 8;
437	                }
438	                pres.insts.push([opTok, ops]);
439	              }
440	            }
441	            q = ds + (csize - 1) * 4;      // 下一子注释（size 含 fourcc token）
442	          }
443	        }
444	        // def 常量内嵌为 token 序列即可；CTAB 符号给寄存器映射
445	        const syms = parseCtab(blob).map((s) => ({
446	          name: s.name, kind: s.regSet === 3 ? 'sampler' : 'const',
447	          reg: s.regSet === 3 ? `s${s.regIndex}` : `c${s.regIndex}`, count: s.regCount,
448	        }));
449	        passesJson.push({ pass: pass.name, obj: oi, tokens, presTokens, preshader: pres, symbols: syms });
450	      }
451	    }
452	  }
453	  writeFileSync(process.argv[jsonIdx + 1], JSON.stringify({ source: file.replace(/^.*\//, ''), passes: passesJson }));
454	  console.log(`json: ${passesJson.length} passes → ${process.argv[jsonIdx + 1]}`);
455	}
456	const out = [];
457	out.push(`== ${file}  params=${numParams} techs=${numTechs} objects=${numObjects} small=${numSmall} large=${numLarge}`);
458	out.push('-- effect params (uniforms):');
459	for (const p of params) {
460	  const v = p.values ? `[${p.values.map((x) => +x.toFixed(4)).join(', ')}]` : p.objects ? `→obj${p.objects}` : p.samplerStates ? 'sampler' : '';
461	  out.push(`  ${p.name ?? '?'} type=${p.type} class=${p.class}${v ? ' ' + v : ''}`);
462	}
463	for (const t of techs) {
464	  out.push(`\n== technique ${t.name} (${t.passes.length} passes)`);
465	  for (const pass of t.passes) {
466	    if (only && !only.has(pass.name)) continue;
467	    out.push(`  -- pass ${pass.name ?? ''}`);
468	    for (const s of pass.states) {
469	      const oi = s.value.objects?.[0];
470	      if (oi === undefined) continue;
471	      if (objType[oi] !== 15 && objType[oi] !== 16) continue;   // D3DXPT PIXELSHADER=15/VERTEXSHADER=16
472	      const blob = blobs.get(oi);
473	      if (!blob) { out.push(`    <missing blob obj${oi}>`); continue; }
474	      out.push(`    <${objType[oi] === 15 ? 'ps' : 'vs'} obj${oi} ${blob.length}B>`);
475	      const dumpIdx = process.argv.indexOf('--dump-obj');
476	      if (dumpIdx >= 0 && process.argv[dumpIdx + 1].split(',').includes(String(oi))) {
477	        writeFileSync(`/tmp/fx-obj${oi}.bin`, blob);
478	        out.push(`    (dumped /tmp/fx-obj${oi}.bin)`);
479	      }
480	      try {
481	        const syms = parseCtab(blob);
482	        out.push(disasm(blob, syms));
483	      } catch (e) { out.push(`    !! ${e.message}`); }
484	    }
485	  }
486	}
487	const text = out.join('\n');
488	writeFileSync(outFile, text);
489	console.log(`wrote ${outFile} (${text.length}B)`);
490	console.log(text.split('\n').slice(0, 40).join('\n'));
491	

```

```js
const s = [sw & 3, (sw >> 2) & 3, (sw >> 4) & 3, (sw >> 6) & 3];
const uniq = s.every((c) => c === s[0]);
return mod + regName(tok) + (uniq ? (s[0] === 0 ? '' : `.${SWZ[s[0]]}`) : `.${s.map((c) => SWZ[c]).join('')}`);
```

So: if all four swizzle components are the same (replicate):
- if replicate-x → print nothing (e.g. `r0` = r0.xxxx)
- else print single letter (e.g. `r0.y` = r0.yyyy)
If not uniform → print all 4 letters (e.g. `.wzyx`).

CRITICAL: this means whenever a non-uniform swizzle is printed, it's ALWAYS printed with all four letters `.abcd`. // ---- 油漆乘色着色画布（ChunkCache 静态烘焙消费，world/Paint.applyPaintTint） ----
// 原版走 GPU shader（TilePaintSystemV2.cs:69-82）；Canvas 2D 用三段合成等价实现：
//   ① 摘出待着色区域 → ② multiply（负相 30 用 difference 反转）填色 →
//   ③ destination-in 按原区域 alpha 裁回（multiply 会把透明像素变成实色，必须裁）
// 全局单例：每 chunk 烘焙是串行的，16×16 复用零分配
const tintCanvas = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.createElement('canvas') : null;
if (tintCanvas) { tintCanvas.width = TILE; tintCanvas.height = TILE; }
const tintCtx = tintCanvas ? tintCanvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true }) : null;  // 纯 CPU scratch:SHM 后备不占 IOSurface(第六场双开 16x16 失败的当事画布)

/** 对 canvas 的 (readX,readY) 16×16 区域按 paint 着色,回写到 ctx 的 (writeX,writeY)。
 *  ★读/写坐标分离(2026-08-18 atlas 化 review 修):atlas 页路径读=页内绝对、写=cell 内
 *  局部(ctx 挂 translate(ox,oy));旧签名读写同参 → 回写落 (ox+px,oy+py) 双重偏移,
 *  ox>0 的 cell 油漆被 clip 挡掉 = 静默丢失(新世界无油漆的对拍探针抓不到) */
function tintRegion(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, src: HTMLCanvasElement, readX: number, readY: number, paint: number,
  writeX = readX, writeY = readY): void {
  if (!tintCtx || !tintCanvas) return;
  tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  tintCtx.clearRect(0, 0, TILE, TILE);
  tintCtx.drawImage(src, readX, readY, TILE, TILE, 0, 0, TILE, TILE);
  if (paint === 30) {
    // 负相漆：difference × 白 = 反转（MapHelper.MapColor :1849-1851 同式）
    tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'difference';
    tintCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  } else {
    // 其余：multiply × paintColor = 逐通道乘色（白漆 26 乘白 ≡ 原色，自然等价）
    tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
    const [tr, tg, tb] = paintColor(paint);
    tintCtx.fillStyle = `rgb(${tr},${tg},${tb})`;
  }
  tintCtx.fillRect(0, 0, TILE, TILE);
  tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
  tintCtx.drawImage(src, readX, readY, TILE, TILE, 0, 0, TILE, TILE);
  tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  // 不 clearRect 直接覆盖回写：着色像素与本区域原内容 alpha 完全一致，
  // source-over 叠加即替换色值；clearRect 反而会打穿邻格溢出的跨格精灵
  ctx.drawImage(tintCanvas, writeX, writeY);
}

Else: 0.75 − 2c8/255, clamp ≥0. c8=0 (but hi>0, e.g. r=100,g=0,b=0): g channel: 0.75 → 191.25 → 191. r: 0.75−0.784 = −0.034 → 0. So wall-negative makes harsh two-tone. OK.

Now — which settings does GetWallSettings return (UseWallShaderHacks)? Let me read TreePaintSystemData.cs fully. Also GetTileSettings for completeness — I'll cite it in docs but implement special-group as a function.

```
// c6=(6,2,4,1/6), c7=(1,0,...)
lo = min(r,g,b); hi = max(r,g,b); delta = hi - lo;
if (delta == 0) { hue = 0; sat = 0; }
else {
  // sector hues: rMax → (g-b)/delta; gMax → 2+(b-r)/delta; bMax → 4+(r-g)/delta
  hue6 = max over channels attaining hi of their sector value (max of the three gated values)
  hue = frac(hue6 / 6)
  sat = delta / hi  (hi>0 since delta>0)
}
hueOff = hue + hueTestOffset;
hue' = hueOff >= 0 ? frac(hueOff) : -frac(-hueOff);
match = (hue' >= leafMinHue) && (hue' <= leafMaxHue) && (sat >= leafMinSat) && (sat <= leafMaxSat);
flag = invert ? !match : match;
out = flag ? paintFormula : original;
```

Now, rounding. Let me define the float pipeline precisely for the JS port:

```js
function fx(r8, g8, b8) {
  const r = r8 / 255, g = g8 / 255, b = b8 / 255;
  const hi = Math.max(r, g, b), lo = Math.min(r, g, b);
  const midS = Math.min(Math.max(r, g), b);
  ... formula ...
  return [to8(outR), to8(outG), to8(outB)];
}
function to8(x) { // UNORM 写回：clamp + 最近偶舍入(GPU UNORM 转换)
  const c = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, x)) * 255;
  const f = Math.floor(c), d = c - f;
  return d < 0.5 ? f : d > 0.5 ? f + 1 : (f % 2 === 0 ? f : f + 1);
}
```

Hmm, actually — regarding GPU rounding: is round-half-even right? For D3D9-era hardware, float→UNORM8 conversion... The D3D11 functional spec says float→fixed conversion uses "round-to-nearest-even" for UNORM? Actually the spec (Direct3D 11.3 functional spec §3.2.2 "Float to Integer Conversion"): "casting a float to an integer... rounds to nearest, ties to even" for the rasterizer; for UNORM output writes, the float is converted via round-to-nearest with ties... hmm honestly implementations round-to-nearest-even. Given ties only arise for exactly representable .5 byte values ((hi+lo)/2 with odd sum, (hi+0.4lo)/2 basically never exact, 0.5(hi+lo) for gray/black/shadow with odd sum), round-half-even gives: (151+106)/2 = 128.5 → 128; JS Math.round → 129. It matters for gray paint on odd-sum pixels. I'll implement half-even with a comment. It's deterministic and documented.

Hmm, wait — actually let me reconsider: are the multiply constants float32-exact? (hi+lo)/2: hi, lo are k/255 — NOT exactly representable in float32 (1/255 is periodic in binary). So (hi8/255 + lo8/255)/2 in float32 ≈ byte value (hi8+lo8)/2/255 with tiny error ~1e-7 — the tie at exactly x.5 becomes x.5 ± ε → rounding resolves by the ε, not by tie rules! Ugh — so the true GPU result at ties depends on float32 rounding of /255 sums. E.g. 151/255 + 106/255: 151/255 = 0.5921568627...; f32(151/255) = 0.592156887...; 106/255 = 0.4156862745; f32 = 0.41568628... sum = 1.007843137... f32 sum; /2 = 0.503921568...; ×255 = 128.5000000xx — the direction depends on the exact f32 bits. This is truly marginal (1 LSB). For the port: compute in double (r8/255 exact-ish in double), formula in double, round half-even. Document the ±1 LSB boundary caveat. The anchors I choose will avoid exact ties so tests are stable regardless.

Actually hmm, let me simplify: since r8/255 in double is very close, and formulas are affine combos of hi/lo in byte units, I can compute directly in byte domain:
- avg_s = (hi8 + lo8) / 2 — but with double math (hi/255 + lo/255)/2 × 255 = (hi8 + lo8)/2 exactly in double. ✓ same.
- lo_d = 0.4 × lo8 — hmm: (0.4f32 × (lo8/255)) × 255 vs 0.4double × lo8: differ by ~1e-7 relative — same rounded byte except razor ties. I'll compute in the normalized domain (r/255 …) to mirror the shader most closely, in double. Fine.

Formula per paint in normalized domain:

```
paint 1: (hi, lo, lo)
paint 2: (hi, (hi+lo)/2, lo)
paint 3: (hi, midS, lo)
paint 4: ((hi+lo)/2, hi, lo)
paint 5: (lo, hi, lo)
paint 6: (lo, hi, (hi+lo)/2)
paint 7: (lo, hi, midS)
paint 8: (lo, (hi+lo)/2, hi)
paint 9: (lo, lo, hi)
paint 10: ((hi+lo)/2, lo, hi)
paint 11: (hi, lo, midS)
paint 12: (hi, lo, (hi+lo)/2)
13-24: same with lo→L=0.4·lo and (hi+lo)/2→(hi+L)/2:
  13: (hi, L, L)
  14: (hi, (hi+L)/2, L)
  15: (hi, midS, L)
  16: ((hi+L)/2, hi, L)
  17: (L, hi, L)
  18: (L, hi, (hi+L)/2)
  19: (L, hi, midS)
  20: (L, (hi+L)/2, hi)
  21: (L, L, hi)
  22: ((hi+L)/2, L, hi)
  23: (hi, L, midS)
  24: (hi, L, (hi+L)/2)
25: g = 0.15(hi+lo) each
26: v = (0.7hi + 0.3lo)(2 − 0.5(hi+lo)) each
27: g = 0.5(hi+lo) each
28: (hi, 0.7hi, 0.49hi)
29: g = 0.025(hi+lo) each
30 tile: c → hi==0 ? c : 1−c

NOT a tie → rounds 9 regardless of tie rule.

If the hw multiplies f32 0.033333335 × f32 255 = f32 8.500000190... → rounds to 9. So GPU → 9. My f32-sim gave 9 ✓, double gave 8 ✗.

r1.y = 0.5
final: out.y = f(r0.y * 0.5) = f(f(hi+lo) * 0.5)

(off-Math.floor(off)) : -((Math.abs(off))-Math.floor(Math.abs(off)));\n  const match = (huep-st.minHue>=0)&&(st.maxHue-huep>=0)&&(sat-st.minSat>=0)&&(st.maxSat-sat>=0);\n  return {flag: st.invert?!match:match, hue, sat, huep, match};\n}\nconst dirt={offset:0,minHue:.03,maxHue:.08,minSat:.38,maxSat:.53,invert:true};\nconsole.log(\"(106,190,48) DefaultDirt:\", JSON.stringify(gate(106,190,48,dirt)));\nconsole.log(\"(200,137,110) DefaultDirt:\", JSON.stringify(gate(200,137,110,dirt)));\n// offset wrap check (CullMud offset=0.5)\nconst mud={offset:.5,minHue:.42,maxHue:.55,minSat:.2,maxSat:.27,invert:true};\nconsole.log(\"(150,100,60) CullMud:\", JSON.stringify(gate(150,100,60,mud)));\n'",
 "description": "Clean leaf-gate simulation for anchors"
}

Formula table approach: for paints 1-12 and 13-24, encode per-paint slot layout. Slots: HI, MID*, LO(shallow/deep), AVG(shallow/deep). Compact representation:

For paint 1-12 / 13-24: slot triple keyed by paint (hue index h = paint<=12 ? paint : paint-12):
- 1: HI, LO, LO
- 2: HI, AVG, LO
- 3: HI, MID, LO
- 4: AVG, HI, LO
- 5: LO, HI, LO
- 6: LO, HI, AVG
- 7: LO, HI, MID

- 9: LO, LO, HI

Now the f32 chain in implementation — mirror instruction order:
```
const hi, lo, midS from cmp chain (no arithmetic — selection only, exact)
const sum = fr(hi + lo)                    // add r1.x, r2, r3.w
const avgS = fr(sum * 0.5f)                // mul r0.?, r1, c0
const lowD = fr(lo * 0.4f)                 // mul r?, r3.w, c0
const sumD = fr(lowD + hi)                 // mad r1.x, r2.w, c0, r0  (mul-then-add f32 序)
const avgD = fr(sumD * 0.5f)
```
WHITE: A = fr(fr(fr(hi*7f) + fr(lo*3f)) * 0.1f); B = fr(fr(sum * -0.5f) + 2f); v = fr(A*B).
BLACK: v = fr(sum * 0.15f). GRAY: v = fr(sum * 0.5f). SHADOW: v = fr(sum * 0.025f).
BROWN: (hi, fr(hi*0.7f), fr(hi*0.49f)).
NEGATIVE tile: hi==0 ? c : fr(1 − c).
NEGATIVE wall: hi==0 ? fr(c+c) : max(fr(0.75f + fr(c * −2)), 0).

Note on the mul by v0: all passes end `mul r0.xyzw, r0.xyzw, v0.xyzw` with v0 = White (1.0) in the bake — exact no-op in f32; skip with comment.

toUnorm8: clamp to [0,1] then ×255, round half-even:
```
function toUnorm8(x: number): number {
  const c = (x < 0 ? 0 : x > 1 ? 1 : x) * 255;
  const fl = Math.floor(c);
  const d = c - fl;
  if (d < 0.5) return fl;
  if (d > 0.5) return fl + 1;
  return fl % 2 === 0 ? fl : fl + 1;   // D3D UNORM 写回: 最近偶(GPU 浮点噪声先破坏平手,此为确定性兜底)
}
```

For applyPaintTintSpecial: hue gate + reuse the slot formula for paints 1-12; paints outside 1-12 → plain formula (ConvertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex only routes 1-12 into special passes).

const PASS_SLOT: readonly [number, number, number][] = [
  [0,2,2],[0,1,2],[0,3,2],[1,0,2],[2,0,2],[2,0,1],[2,0,3],[2,1,0],[2,2,0],[1,2,0],[0,2,3],[0,2,1],
]; // index = paint-1
```

The gate in f32 chain:
```ts
const rf = fr32(r / 255), gf = fr32(g / 255), bf = fr32(b / 255);
const lo = Math.min(rf, gf, bf), hi = Math.max(rf, gf, bf);
const delta = fr32(hi - lo);
let hue = 0, sat = 0;
if (delta !== 0) {
  const invD = fr32(1 / delta);
  const secR = fr32(fr32(fr32(gf - bf) * invD) + 6);   // 红支: (g-b)/Δ + 6（+6≡+0 mod 6，负值归一）
  const secG = fr32(fr32(fr32(bf - rf) * invD) + 2);
  const secB = fr32(fr32(fr32(rf - gf) * invD) + 4);
  const hue6 = Math.max(rf >= hi ? secR : 0, Math.max(gf >= hi ? secG : 0, bf >= hi ? secB : 0));
  const t = fr32(hue6 * fr32(1 / 6));
  hue = fr32(t - Math.floor(t));
  sat = fr32(delta * fr32(1 / hi));
}
const off = fr32(hue + s.hueTestOffset);
const huep = off >= 0 ? fr32(off - Math.floor(off)) : -fr32(Math.abs(off) - Math.floor(Math.abs(off)));
```

Hmm wait — the shader's frc: `frc r1.z, r1.z` where r1.z = abs(off). D3D frc on f32 = x − floor(x) — result is f32-rounded. Then cmp negates: `-r1.z` — negation exact. So huep = off >= 0 ? frc32(off) : −frc32(|off|). My formula: frc32(x) = fr32(x − Math.floor(x)) ✓.

Careful: the shader computed hue via `mul r1.y, r3.z, c6.w` = hue6 × (1/6 f32). f32(1/6) = 0.16666667163372. Then frc. ✓ as implemented.

Also indicator comparisons: `add r2.yzw, r0.wzyx, -r2` computes (c − hi); cmp cond = (c − hi) >= 0 ⟺ c >= hi (f32 subtraction of equal values = exactly 0 ✓). So indicator = c >= hi (inclusive, catches ties). ✓.

But hmm — `add r2.yzw, r0.wzyx, -r2`: computed in f32: f(c − hi). If c == hi exactly → 0 → >= 0 true ✓. If c < hi → negative ✓. No rounding hazard (c − hi exact by Sterbenz? Not necessarily exact, but sign is preserved: f32 subtraction rounds to nearest, can't flip sign of nonzero, and equal values give exactly 0). ✓.

sat: `rcp r2.x, r2` = f32 reciprocal 1/hi (rounded); `mul r3.y, r1, r2` = f(Δ × f(1/hi)). ✓.

Match comparisons in f32: (huep − minHue) >= 0 — the shader: `add r1.x, r1, -c0` then cmp — f32 sub then compare >= 0 ✓. Uniform c0-c5 are f32 on GPU (SetValue float → f32). TS: the settings numbers are doubles; do the subtraction in f32: `fr32(huep - s.minHue) >= 0`. Actually the cmp compares the f32 result of the add — I'll wrap with fr32 to mirror.

Edge: what about `add r1.z, -r1.y, c3` = f(leafMaxSat − sat)? order: c3 − sat. I'll mirror operand order: fr32(s.maxSat - sat) >= 0.

Now `invert` handling: shader does `cmp r1.x, -c5, r1, r1.y` — c5 = invertSpecialGroupResult set via SetValue(bool→float 0/1). cond = −c5 >= 0 ⟺ invert <= 0 ⟺ !invert. flag = !invert ? match : !match ✓.

Implementation of the preamble (selection, exact):
```ts
const rf = fr32(r / 255), gf = fr32(g / 255), bf = fr32(b / 255);
const mMax = Math.max(rf, gf);        // max r1.x, r0, r0.y
const mMin = Math.min(gf, rf);        // min r1.y, r0.y, r0
const cond = mMax - bf;               // add r1.w, -r0.z, r1
const hi = cond >= 0 ? mMax : bf;     // cmp r2.x
const midS = cond >= 0 ? bf : mMax;   // cmp r2.y
const lo = Math.min(midS, mMin);      // min r3.w, r2.y, r1.y
```

Hmm — `mMax - bf` in double vs f32: cond's sign only matters; double subtraction sign is exact (no rounding flips sign; equal values → 0) ✓ no need to fround.

For paint 30, the preamble differs! Pass43/44 use the 2-instr 3-max: `max r1.w, r0, r0.y; max r2.w, r1.w, r0.z` → hi = max3. Same value ✓. (And Pass41 uses the same 2-instr version.)

Also for paint 30: `add r1.x, -r0, c0` → f(1 − c). In TS: fr32(1 - rf)? Hmm — c0 = 1.0f; the add computes 1.0 − c in f32. My sim used f(1−c) ✓.

```ts
/** float32 一步舍入（SM2 寄存器精度;逐指令镜像字节码运算序） */
const fr32 = Math.fround;

/** UNORM 写回：clamp [0,1] → ×255 最近偶取整（GPU 渲染目标 8bit 量化;
 *  f32 运算噪声通常先破坏平手,此为确定性兜底） */
function unorm8(x: number): number {
  const c = (x < 0 ? 0 : x > 1 ? 1 : x) * 255;
  const fl = Math.floor(c);
  const d = c - fl;
  return d < 0.5 ? fl : d > 0.5 ? fl + 1 : fl + (fl % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 1);
}

/** ConvertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex（Main.cs:58064-58083 逐行 1:1）:
 *  paint → TileShader.cso technique Passes[] 序号(0 起,Pass1=0)。
 *  useSpecialGroups=isUsedForPaintingGrass / useWallShaderHacks=墙 bake 恒 true
 *  (TreePaintSystemData.GetWallSettings :399-402 → DefaultNoSpecialGroups_ForWalls)。 */
export function convertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex(
  paintIndexOnTile: number, isUsedForPaintingGrass: boolean, useWallShaderHacks: boolean,
): number {
  if (paintIndexOnTile === 31) return 0;                       // :58068-58071
  if (paintIndexOnTile === 30 && useWallShaderHacks) return 43; // :58072-58075
  if (paintIndexOnTile >= 28) return paintIndexOnTile + 12;     // :58076-58079 棕28/暗影29/负相30
  if (isUsedForPaintingGrass && paintIndexOnTile >= 1 && paintIndexOnTile <= 12) {
    return paintIndexOnTile + 27;                               // :58080-58083 特判组
  }
  return paintIndexOnTile;                                      // 0-27 直映
}
```

  // 公共前导(Pass2-28/:42-42... 略) — 31/30 两式见负相分支

}

```ts
  const mMax = Math.max(rf, gf), mMin = Math.min(gf, rf);
  const hi = mMax >= bf ? mMax : bf;         // cmp r2.x(:30/:34)
  const midS = mMax >= bf ? bf : mMax;       // cmp r2.y(:31/:35) 部分排序中位槽
  const lo = Math.min(midS, mMin);           // min r3.w(:36/:55) = min3
  const sum = fr32(hi + lo);                 // add r1.x(:56) 黑/白/灰/暗影共用
  const avgS = fr32(sum * fr32(0.5));        // mul c0 0.5(:60)
  const lowD = fr32(lo * fr32(0.4));         // mul c0 0.4(深层低槽)
  const sumD = fr32(lowD + hi);              // mad c0(深层 avg 分子, mul-then-add 序)
  const avgD = fr32(sumD * fr32(0.5));
  switch (paint) {
    case 1: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo), unorm8(lo)];              // Pass2 :23-40 RED (hi,lo,lo)
    case 2: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgS), unorm8(lo)];            // Pass3 :41-63 ORANGE
    case 3: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS), unorm8(lo)];            // Pass4 :64-81 YELLOW g=midS
    case 4: return [unorm8(avgS), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo)];            // Pass5 :82-104 LIME
    case 5: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo)];              // Pass6 :105-122 GREEN
    case 6: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgS)];            // Pass7 :123-145 TEAL
    case 7: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS)];            // Pass8 :146-163 CYAN b=midS
    case 8: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(avgS), unorm8(hi)];            // Pass9 :164-186 SKY_BLUE
    case 9: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi)];              // Pass10 :187-204 BLUE
    case 10: return [unorm8(avgS), unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi)];           // Pass11 :205-227 PURPLE
    case 11: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo), unorm8(midS)];           // Pass12 :228-245 VIOLET b=midS
    case 12: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo), unorm8(avgS)];           // Pass13 :246-268 PINK
    case 13: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(lowD)];         // Pass14 :269-287 DEEP_RED
    case 14: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgD), unorm8(lowD)];         // Pass15 :288-310 DEEP_ORANGE
    case 15: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS), unorm8(lowD)];         // Pass16 :311-332 DEEP_YELLOW
    case 16: return [unorm8(avgD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD)];         // Pass17 :333-356 DEEP_LIME
    case 17: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD)];         // Pass18 :357-376 DEEP_GREEN
    case 18: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgD)];         // Pass19 :377-400 DEEP_TEAL
    case 19: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS)];         // Pass20 :401-422 DEEP_CYAN
    case 20: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(avgD), unorm8(hi)];         // Pass21 :423-446 DEEP_SKY_BLUE
    case 21: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi)];         // Pass22 :447-466 DEEP_BLUE
    case 22: return [unorm8(avgD), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi)];         // Pass23 :467-490 DEEP_PURPLE
    case 23: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(midS)];         // Pass24 :491-512 DEEP_VIOLET
    case 24: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(avgD)];         // Pass25 :513-535 DEEP_PINK
    case 25: { const v = unorm8(fr32(sum * fr32(0.15))); return [v, v, v]; }   // Pass26 :536-555 BLACK
    case 26: {                                                            // Pass27 :556-580 WHITE
      const A = fr32(fr32(fr32(hi * 7) + fr32(lo * 3)) * fr32(0.1));    // mul c1.x=3 / mad c1.y=7 / mul c1.z=0.1
      const B = fr32(fr32(sum * -0.5) + 2);                             // mad -c0.x=0.5, c0.y=2
      const v = unorm8(fr32(A * B));
      return [v, v, v];
    }
    case 27: { const v = unorm8(avgS); return [v, v, v]; }              // Pass28 :581-600 GRAY(同式 0.5(hi+lo))
    case 28: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(fr32(hi * fr32(0.7))), unorm8(fr32(hi * fr32(0.49)))]; // Pass41 :1321-1338 BROWN
    case 29: { const v = unorm8(fr32(sum * fr32(0.025))); return [v, v, v]; } // Pass42 :1339-1358 SHADOW
    case 30:                                                             // Pass43 :1359-1377 / Pass44 :1378-1402
      if (isWall) {
        const w = (c: number) => unorm8(Math.max(hi === 0 ? fr32(c + c) : fr32(fr32(0.75) + fr32(c * -2)), 0));
        return [w(rf), w(gf), w(bf)];
      }
      return [hi === 0 ? r : unorm8(fr32(1 - rf)), hi === 0 ? g : unorm8(fr32(1 - gf)), hi === 0 ? b : unorm8(fr32(1 - bf))];
  }
```

Wait case 27: GRAY = f(sum × 0.5f) = avgS ✓ (same). Keep `avgS` but comment.

Hmm — case 30 negative: when hi==0 the pixel is (0,0,0) → return r,g,b = 0s anyway. `hi === 0 ? r : ...` returns ORIGINAL ints r,g,b (which are 0). ✓ literal `cmp` selects original.

Note on paint 30 wall: `fr32(0.75)` is just 0.75 exactly representable ✓. `c * -2` — shader: mad r1.y, r0, -c0, c0.y = f(f(c × −2.0) + 0.75). I wrote fr32(fr32(0.75) + fr32(c * -2)) — same.

One subtlety — `hi === 0` check: hi is f32; for black pixel rf=gf=bf=0 → mMax=0, hi=0 ✓. For non-black, hi > 0. But what about extremely small values? Inputs are byte/255 so hi ∈ {0} ∪ [1/255, 1]. ✓.

But careful: the wall/black branch `fr32(c + c)`: shader `add r1.x, r0, r0` = f(c+c) ✓.

Now `applyPaintTintSpecial`:

```ts
/** 特判组(叶色相)pass 参数(TreePaintSystemData.cs TreePaintingSettings 字段映射;
 *  具体每 type/style 的取值见该文件 :16-243,墙恒 DefaultNoSpecialGroups_ForWalls)。 */
export interface LeafPaintSettings {
  /** leafHueTestOffset(HueTestOffset;CullMud/PalmTreeCrimson=0.5) */
  hueTestOffset: number;
  /** leafMinHue/leafMaxHue(SpecialGroupMinimal/MaximumHueValue) */
  minHue: number;
  maxHue: number;
  /** leafMinSat/leafMaxSat(SpecialGroupMinimum/MaximumSaturationValue) */
  minSat: number;
  maxSat: number;
  /** invertSpecialGroupResult(InvertSpecialGroupResult) */
  invert: boolean;
}
```

And:

```ts
export function applyPaintTintSpecial(
  r: number, g: number, b: number, paint: number, s: LeafPaintSettings,
): readonly [number, number, number] {
  if (paint < 1 || paint > 12) return applyPaintTint(r, g, b, paint); // 特判支仅 1-12(Main.cs:58080)
  const rf = fr32(r / 255), gf = fr32(g / 255), bf = fr32(b / 255);
  const lo = Math.min(rf, gf, bf), hi = Math.max(rf, gf, bf);
  const delta = fr32(hi - lo);            // add r1.x, -r2.w, r2 (:614)
  let hue = 0, sat = 0;                   // cmp -r1(:632): Δ==0 → hue=sat=0
  if (delta !== 0) {
    const invD = fr32(1 / delta);         // rcp r1.y(:615)
    // 三扇区色相(基数 R:+6≡0 G:+2 B:+4;:616-621):分量配对 r1.w=R支 r1.z=G支 r1.y=B支
    const secR = fr32(fr32(fr32(gf - bf) * invD) + 6);  // (g-b)/Δ
    const secG = fr32(fr32(fr32(bf - rf) * invD) + 2);  // (b-r)/Δ
    const secB = fr32(fr32(fr32(rf - gf) * invD) + 4);  // (r-g)/Δ
    // 逐通道达标指示(:622 cmp r0.wzyx vs -r2 => c==hi) → 三选一 max(:627-629)
    const hue6 = Math.max(
      bf >= hi ? secB : 0,
      Math.max(gf >= hi ? secG : 0, rf >= hi ? secR : 0),
    );
    const t = fr32(hue6 * fr32(1 / 6));   // mul c6.w=1/6(:630)
    hue = fr32(t - Math.floor(t));        // frc(:631)
    sat = fr32(delta * fr32(1 / hi));     // rcp r2(:623) + mul(:624) = Δ/hi(HSV 饱和)
  }
  // hue + offset 的带符号回绕(:636-640): >=0 取 frc,<0 取 -frc(|x|)
  const off = fr32(hue + s.hueTestOffset);
  const huep = off >= 0
    ? fr32(off - Math.floor(off))
    : -fr32(Math.abs(off) - Math.floor(Math.abs(off)));
  // 四门全含边界(:633-644,均为 >=0 即闭区间)
  const match = fr32(huep - s.minHue) >= 0 && fr32(s.maxHue - huep) >= 0
    && fr32(sat - s.minSat) >= 0 && fr32(s.maxSat - sat) >= 0;
  const flag = s.invert ? !match : match;  // cmp -c5(:645-646)
  return flag ? applyPaintTint(r, g, b, paint) : [r, g, b]; // cmp -r1(:655)
}
```

Hmm wait — `applyPaintTint` returns unorm8 outputs. And flag=false → original bytes [r,g,b] ✓ (Pass29: `cmp r0.xyz, -r1, r0.xyzw, r4.xyzw` picks original texel → note original = the UNORM input = exact bytes ✓).

`Math.abs(off)` — off < 0 → |off| = −off exactly ✓.

frc of |off| — `frc r1.z, r1.z` where r1.z = abs(off) ✓.

ChunkCache.tintRegion（运行期实际着色路径）仍是 multiply 乘色合成——该文件归\n//      并行会话管，本文件先行落真值；接线时以本函数逐像素替换（ImageData 循环，\n//      不再整矩形 fill）。\n//   3. 精度：逐指令按 f32 寄存器序模拟（Math.fround）；老 ATI fp24 硬件与 fp32 存在\n//      LSB 级差；写回按 UNORM8 最近偶取整兜底。\n//   4. 涂层（paintCoating 1/2 → fullbright/invisible 位）系统未建，另行登记（文件头）。\n\n/** float32 一步舍入（SM2 寄存器精度——逐指令镜像字节码运算序） */\nconst fr32 = Math.fround;\n\n/** UNORM 写回：clamp [0,1] → ×255 最近偶取整（渲染目标 8bit 量化；\n *  f32 运算噪声通常先破坏平手值，此为确定性兜底，与 GPU 至多差 1 LSB） */\nfunction unorm8(x: number): number {\n  const c = (x < 0 ? 0 : x > 1 ? 1 : x) * 255;\n  const fl = Math.floor(c);\n  const d = c - fl;\n  return d < 0.5 ? fl : d > 0.5 ? fl + 1 : fl + (fl % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 1);\n}\n\n/** ConvertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex（Main.cs:58064-58083 逐行 1:1）：\n *  paint → TileShader.cso Technique1.Passes[] 序号（0 起，Pass1=0）。\n *  isUsedForPaintingGrass=特判组（TreePaintingSettings.UseSpecialGroups）；\n *  useWallShaderHacks=墙 bake 恒 true（TreePaintSystemData.cs:399-402）。 */\nexport function convertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex(\n  paintIndexOnTile: number, isUsedForPaintingGrass: boolean, useWallShaderHacks: boolean,\n): number {\n  if (paintIndexOnTile === 31) return 0;                        // :58068-58071\n  if (paintIndexOnTile === 30 && useWallShaderHacks) return 43; // :58072-58075\n  if (paintIndexOnTile >= 28) return paintIndexOnTile + 12;     // :58076-58079 棕/暗影/负相\n  if (isUsedForPaintingGrass && paintIndexOnTile >= 1 && paintIndexOnTile <= 12) {\n    return paintIndexOnTile + 27;                               // :58080-58083 特判组\n  }\n  return paintIndexOnTile;                                      // 0-27 直映\n}\n\n/**\n * 油漆像素调制：把 tile 原色 (r,g,b) 按 paint id 调制，返回新的 [r,g,b]。\n * TileShader.cso SM2 字节码逐指令译出（公式总表与行号见本节头注释）。\n * isWall=true 走负相墙档 Pass44（GetWallShaderHacks；其余 paint 墙/瓦片同 pass）。\n * paint 0/31（废弃荧光）/越界 → 恒等（Pass1）。\n */\nexport function applyPaintTint(\n  r: number, g: number, b: number, paint: number, isWall = false,\n): readonly [number, number, number] {\n  if (paint <= 0 || paint > 30) return [r, g, b]; // Pass1 :13-22（texld×v0 恒等）\n  const rf = fr32(r / 255), gf = fr32(g / 255), bf = fr32(b / 255); // texld r0（UNORM→f32）\n  // 排序槽前导（Pass41/43/44 用两条 max 求 hi，值同；midS/lo 仅 2-28 消费）\n  const mMax = Math.max(rf, gf);            // max r1.x :30\n  const mMin = Math.min(gf, rf);            // min r1.y :31\n  const hi = mMax >= bf ? mMax : bf;        // cmp r2.x :34 = max3\n  const midS = mMax >= bf ? bf : mMax;      // cmp r2.y :35 = min(max(r,g),b)\n  const lo = Math.min(midS, mMin);          // min r3.w :36 = min3\n  const sum = fr32(hi + lo);                // add r1.x :552/:573/:596（黑/白/灰/暗影分子）\n  const avgS = fr32(sum * fr32(0.5));       // mul c0 0.5（浅层 0.5 档）\n  const lowD = fr32(lo * fr32(0.4));        // mul c0 0.4（深层低槽，def c0 :272 等）\n  const sumD = fr32(lowD + hi);             // mad r1.x, r2.w, c0, r0（mul-then-add 序）\n  const avgD = fr32(sumD * fr32(0.5));      // 深层 0.5 档\n  switch (paint) {\n    case 1: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo), unorm8(lo)];        // Pass2 :23-40 红\n    case 2: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgS), unorm8(lo)];      // Pass3 :41-63 橙\n    case 3: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS), unorm8(lo)];      // Pass4 :64-81 黄（g=midS）\n    case 4: return [unorm8(avgS), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo)];      // Pass5 :82-104 菜\n    case 5: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo)];        // Pass6 :105-122 绿\n    case 6: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgS)];      // Pass7 :123-145 鸭\n    case 7: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS)];      // Pass8 :146-163 青（b=midS）\n    case 8: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(avgS), unorm8(hi)];      // Pass9 :164-186 天蓝\n    case 9: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi)];        // Pass10 :187-204 蓝\n    case 10: return [unorm8(avgS), unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi)];     // Pass11 :205-227 紫\n    case 11: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo), unorm8(midS)];     // Pass12 :228-245 紫红（b=midS）\n    case 12: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo), unorm8(avgS)];     // Pass13 :246-268 粉\n    case 13: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass14 :269-287 深红\n    case 14: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgD), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass15 :288-310 深橙\n    case 15: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass16 :311-332 深黄（g=midS）\n    case 16: return [unorm8(avgD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass17 :333-356 深菜\n    case 17: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass18 :357-376 深绿\n    case 18: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgD)];   // Pass19 :377-400 深鸭\n    case 19: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS)];   // Pass20 :401-422 深青（b=midS）\n    case 20: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(avgD), unorm8(hi)];   // Pass21 :423-446 深天蓝\n    case 21: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi)];   // Pass22 :447-466 深蓝\n    case 22: return [unorm8(avgD), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi)];   // Pass23 :467-490 深紫\n    case 23: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(midS)];   // Pass24 :491-512 深紫红（b=midS）\n    case 24: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(avgD)];   // Pass25 :513-535 深粉\n    case 25: { // Pass26 :536-555 黑：0.15(hi+lo)\n      const v = unorm8(fr32(sum * fr32(0.15)));\n      return [v, v, v];\n    }\n    case 26: { // Pass27 :556-580 白：(7hi+3lo)·0.1 × (2−0.5(hi+lo))——提亮曲线非恒等!\n      const A = fr32(fr32(fr32(hi * 7) + fr32(lo * 3)) * fr32(0.1)); // c1=(3,7,0.1) :572-575\n      const B = fr32(fr32(sum * -0.5) + 2);                          // c0=(0.5,2) :576\n      const v = unorm8(fr32(A * B));\n      return [v, v, v];\n    }\n    case 27: { // Pass28 :581-600 灰：0.5(hi+lo)（= avgS 同式）\n      const v = unorm8(avgS);\n      return [v, v, v];\n    }\n    case 28: // Pass41 :1321-1338 棕：(hi, 0.7hi, 0.49hi)（c0=(0,0.7,0.49)，只吃 hi 不吃 lo）\n      return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(fr32(hi * fr32(0.7))), unorm8(fr32(hi * fr32(0.49)))];\n    case 29: { // Pass42 :1339-1358 暗影：0.025(hi+lo)\n      const v = unorm8(fr32(sum * fr32(0.025)));\n      return [v, v, v];\n    }\n    case 30: { // Pass43 :1359-1377 / Pass44 :1378-1402\n      if (isWall) { // 墙档：max(hi==0 ? 2c : 1.5−2c, 0)\n        const w = (c: number) => unorm8(Math.max(\n          hi === 0 ? fr32(c + c) : fr32(fr32(0.75) + fr32(c * -2)), 0));\n        return [w(rf), w(gf), w(bf)];\n      }\n      // 瓦片档：hi==0（纯黑）守卫——黑像素保持黑（透明边/描线不反白）\n      return [\n        hi === 0 ? r : unorm8(fr32(1 - rf)),\n        hi === 0 ? g : unorm8(fr32(1 - gf)),\n        hi === 0 ? b : unorm8(fr32(1 - bf)),\n      ];\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** 特判组（叶色相）pass 参数——TreePaintingSettings 字段映射\n *  （TilePaintSystemV2.cs:72-77 置 leafHue* 五参；每 type/style 取值见\n *  TreePaintSystemData.cs:16-243，墙恒 DefaultNoSpecialGroups_ForWalls :10-14）。 */\nexport interface LeafPaintSettings {\n  /** leafHueTestOffset（HueTestOffset；CullMud/PalmTreeCrimson = 0.5） */\n  hueTestOffset: number;\n  /** leafMinHue / leafMaxHue（SpecialGroupMinimal/MaximumHueValue） */\n  minHue: number;\n  maxHue: number;\n  /** leafMinSat / leafMaxSat（SpecialGroupMinimum/MaximumSaturationValue） */\n  minSat: number;\n  maxSat: number;\n  /** invertSpecialGroupResult（InvertSpecialGroupResult） */\n  invert: boolean;\n}\n\n/**\n * 特判组油漆调制（Pass29-40 = 浅层槽位式 + 逐像素 hue/sat 门，fxTile :601-1320）。\n * ConvertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex 特判支仅覆盖 paint 1-12（:58080-58083）——\n * 其余 id 直接回落普通 applyPaintTint（含深层 13-24：特判组不吃深层，原版语义）。\n * 门为假 → 返回原色（cmp r0.xyz, -r1, r0, r4 :655 选中支）。\n */\nexport function applyPaintTintSpecial(\n  r: number, g: number, b: number, paint: number, s: LeafPaintSettings,\n): readonly [number, number, number] {\n  if (paint < 1 || paint > 12) return applyPaintTint(r, g, b, paint);\n  const rf = fr32(r / 255), gf = fr32(g / 255), bf = fr32(b / 255);\n  const lo = Math.min(rf, gf, bf);   // min r1.w/r2.w :610-611\n  const hi = Math.max(rf, gf, bf);   // max r1.x/r2.x :612-613\n  const delta = fr32(hi - lo);       // add r1.x, -r2.w, r2 :614\n  let hue = 0, sat = 0;              // cmp -r1（−Δ²≥0 ⟺ Δ==0）:625/:632 → 灰度像素归零\n  if (delta !== 0) {\n    const invD = fr32(1 / delta);    // rcp r1.y :615\n    // 三扇区色相（HSV 基数 R:+6(≡0,负值归一) G:+2 B:+4；:616-621 通道配对后 r1.w/z/y）\n    const secR = fr32(fr32(fr32(gf - bf) * invD) + 6); // (g−b)/Δ\n    const secG = fr32(fr32(fr32(bf - rf) * invD) + 2); // (b−r)/Δ\n    const secB = fr32(fr32(fr32(rf - gf) * invD) + 4); // (r−g)/Δ\n    // 达标指示（c−hi ≥ 0 ⟺ c==max，并列取大 hue；:622 cmp）→ 三选一（:627-629 max 链）\n    const hue6 = Math.max(\n      bf >= hi ? secB : 0,\n      Math.max(gf >= hi ? secG : 0, rf >= hi ? secR : 0),\n    );\n    const t = fr32(hue6 * fr32(1 / 6)); // mul c6.w=1/6 :630\n    hue = fr32(t - Math.floor(t));      // frc :631\n    sat = fr32(delta * fr32(1 / hi));   // rcp r2 :623 + mul :624 = Δ/hi（HSV 饱和度）\n  }\n  // hue + hueTestOffset 的【带符号回绕】（:636-640）：≥0 取 frc(x)，<0 取 −frc(|x|)\n  // （≠数学 frc：负侧映射到 (−1,0)，使窗比较可跨 0 环两侧取值）\n  const off = fr32(hue + s.hueTestOffset);\n  const huep = off >= 0\n    ? fr32(off - Math.floor(off))\n    : -fr32(Math.abs(off) - Math.floor(Math.abs(off)));\n  // 四门全闭区间（:633-644 均为 ≥0 判据）：hue∈[min,max] ∧ sat∈[min,max]\n  const match = fr32(huep - s.minHue) >= 0 && fr32(s.maxHue - huep) >= 0\n    && fr32(sat - s.minSat) >= 0 && fr32(s.maxSat - sat) >= 0;\n  const flag = s.invert ? !match : match; // cmp −c5 :645-646\n  return flag ? applyPaintTint(r, g, b, paint) : [r, g, b];\n}"
}

177	const fr32 = Math.fround;

179	/** UNORM 写回：clamp [0,1] → ×255 最近偶取整（渲染目标 8bit 量化；
180	 *  f32 运算噪声通常先破坏平手值，此为确定性兜底，与 GPU 至多差 1 LSB） */
181	function unorm8(x: number): number {
182	  const c = (x < 0 ? 0 : x > 1 ? 1 : x) * 255;
183	  const fl = Math.floor(c);
184	  const d = c - fl;
185	  return d < 0.5 ? fl : d > 0.5 ? fl + 1 : fl + (fl % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 1);
186	}

192	export function convertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex(
193	  paintIndexOnTile: number, isUsedForPaintingGrass: boolean, useWallShaderHacks: boolean,
194	): number {

196	  if (paintIndexOnTile === 30 && useWallShaderHacks) return 43; // :58072-58075
197	  if (paintIndexOnTile >= 28) return paintIndexOnTile + 12;     // :58076-58079 棕/暗影/负相
198	  if (isUsedForPaintingGrass && paintIndexOnTile >= 1 && paintIndexOnTile <= 12) {
199	    return paintIndexOnTile + 27;                               // :58080-58083 特判组
200	  }
201	  return paintIndexOnTile;                                      // 0-27 直映
202	}

204	/**
205	 * 油漆像素调制：把 tile 原色 (r,g,b) 按 paint id 调制，返回新的 [r,g,b]。
206	 * TileShader.cso SM2 字节码逐指令译出（公式总表与行号见本节头注释）。
207	 * isWall=true 走负相墙档 Pass44（GetWallShaderHacks；其余 paint 墙/瓦片同 pass）。
208	 * paint 0/31（废弃荧光）/越界 → 恒等（Pass1）。
209	 */
210	export function applyPaintTint(
211	  r: number, g: number, b: number, paint: number, isWall = false,
212	): readonly [number, number, number] {
213	  if (paint <= 0 || paint > 30) return [r, g, b]; // Pass1 :13-22（texld×v0 恒等）
214	  const rf = fr32(r / 255), gf = fr32(g / 255), bf = fr32(b / 255); // texld r0（UNORM→f32）
215	  // 排序槽前导（Pass41/43/44 用两条 max 求 hi，值同；midS/lo 仅 2-28 消费）
216	  const mMax = Math.max(rf, gf);            // max r1.x :30
217	  const mMin = Math.min(gf, rf);            // min r1.y :31
218	  const hi = mMax >= bf ? mMax : bf;        // cmp r2.x :34 = max3
219	  const midS = mMax >= bf ? bf : mMax;      // cmp r2.y :35 = min(max(r,g),b)
220	  const lo = Math.min(midS, mMin);          // min r3.w :36 = min3
221	  const sum = fr32(hi + lo);                // add r1.x :551/:573/:596（黑/白/灰/暗影分子）
222	  const avgS = fr32(sum * fr32(0.5));       // mul c0 0.5（浅层 0.5 档）
223	  const lowD = fr32(lo * fr32(0.4));        // mul c0 0.4（深层低槽，def c0 :272 等）
224	  const sumD = fr32(lowD + hi);             // mad r1.x, r2.w, c0, r0（mul-then-add 序）
225	  const avgD = fr32(sumD * fr32(0.5));      // 深层 0.5 档
226	  switch (paint) {
227	    case 1: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo), unorm8(lo)];        // Pass2 :23-40 红
228	    case 2: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgS), unorm8(lo)];      // Pass3 :41-63 橙
229	    case 3: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS), unorm8(lo)];      // Pass4 :64-81 黄（g=midS）
230	    case 4: return [unorm8(avgS), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo)];      // Pass5 :82-104 青柠
231	    case 5: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo)];        // Pass6 :105-122 绿
232	    case 6: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgS)];      // Pass7 :123-145 蓝绿
233	    case 7: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS)];      // Pass8 :146-163 青（b=midS）
234	    case 8: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(avgS), unorm8(hi)];      // Pass9 :164-186 天蓝
235	    case 9: return [unorm8(lo), unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi)];        // Pass10 :187-204 蓝
236	    case 10: return [unorm8(avgS), unorm8(lo), unorm8(hi)];     // Pass11 :205-227 紫
237	    case 11: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo), unorm8(midS)];     // Pass12 :228-245 紫罗兰（b=midS）
238	    case 12: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lo), unorm8(avgS)];     // Pass13 :246-268 粉
239	    case 13: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass14 :269-287 深红
240	    case 14: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgD), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass15 :288-310 深橙
241	    case 15: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass16 :311-332 深黄（g=midS）
242	    case 16: return [unorm8(avgD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass17 :333-356 深青柠
243	    case 17: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD)];   // Pass18 :357-376 深绿
244	    case 18: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(avgD)];   // Pass19 :377-400 深蓝绿
245	    case 19: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi), unorm8(midS)];   // Pass20 :401-422 深青（b=midS）
246	    case 20: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(avgD), unorm8(hi)];   // Pass21 :423-446 深天蓝
247	    case 21: return [unorm8(lowD), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi)];   // Pass22 :447-466 深蓝
248	    case 22: return [unorm8(avgD), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(hi)];   // Pass23 :467-490 深紫
249	    case 23: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(midS)];   // Pass24 :491-512 深紫罗兰（b=midS）
250	    case 24: return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(lowD), unorm8(avgD)];   // Pass25 :513-535 深粉
251	    case 25: { // Pass26 :536-555 黑：0.15(hi+lo)
252	      const v = unorm8(fr32(sum * fr32(0.15)));
253	      return [v, v, v];
254	    }
255	    case 26: { // Pass27 :556-580 白：(7hi+3lo)·0.1 × (2−0.5(hi+lo))——提亮曲线非恒等!
256	      const A = fr32(fr32(fr32(hi * 7) + fr32(lo * 3)) * fr32(0.1)); // c1=(3,7,0.1) :572-575
257	      const B = fr32(fr32(sum * -0.5) + 2);                          // c0=(0.5,2) :576
258	      const v = unorm8(fr32(A * B));
259	      return [v, v, v];
260	    }
261	    case 27: { // Pass28 :581-600 灰：0.5(hi+lo)（= avgS 同式）
262	      const v = unorm8(avgS);
263	      return [v, v, v];
264	    }
265	    case 28: // Pass41 :1321-1338 棕：(hi, 0.7hi, 0.49hi)（c0=(0,0.7,0.49)，只吃 hi 不吃 lo）
266	      return [unorm8(hi), unorm8(fr32(hi * fr32(0.7))), unorm8(fr32(hi * fr32(0.49)))];
267	    case 29: { // Pass42 :1339-1358 暗影：0.025(hi+lo)
268	      const v = unorm8(fr32(sum * fr32(0.025)));
269	      return [v, v, v];

272	      if (isWall) { // 墙档：max(hi==0 ? 2c : 1.5−2c, 0)
273	        const w = (c: number) => unorm8(Math.max(

276	      }
277	      // 瓦片档：hi==0（纯黑）守卫——黑像素保持黑（透明边/描线不反白）
278	      return [
279	        hi === 0 ? r : unorm8(fr32(1 - rf)),
280	        hi === 0 ? g : unorm8(fr32(1 - gf)),
281	        hi === 0 ? b : unorm8(fr32(1 - bf)),
282	      ];
283	    }
284	    default:
285	      return [r, g, b]; // 不可达（顶部门 1-30；TS 穷尽性兜底）
286	  }

289	/** 特判组（叶色相）pass 参数——TreePaintingSettings 字段映射
290	 *  （TilePaintSystemV2.cs:72-77 置 leafHue* 五参；每 type/style 取值见
291	 *  TreePaintSystemData.cs:16-243，墙恒 DefaultNoSpecialGroups_ForWalls :10-14）。 */
292	export interface LeafPaintSettings {
293	  /** leafHueTestOffset（HueTestOffset；CullMud/PalmTreeCrimson = 0.5） */

306	 * 特判组油漆调制（Pass29-40 = 浅层槽位式 + 逐像素 hue/sat 门，fxTile :601-1320）。
307	 * ConvertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex 特判支仅覆盖 paint 1-12（:58080-58083）——
308	 * 其余 id 直接回落普通 applyPaintTint（含深层 13-24：特判组不吃深层，原版语义）。
309	 * 门为假 → 返回原色（cmp r0.xyz, -r1, r0, r4 :655 选中支）。
310	 */
311	export function applyPaintTintSpecial(
312	  r: number, g: number, b: number, paint: number, s: LeafPaintSettings,
313	): readonly [number, number, number] {
314	  if (paint < 1 || paint > 12) return applyPaintTint(r, g, b, paint);
315	  const rf = fr32(r / 255), gf = fr32(g / 255), bf = fr32(b / 255);
316	  const lo = Math.min(rf, gf, bf);   // min r1.w/r2.w :610-611
317	  const hi = Math.max(rf, gf, bf);   // max r1.x/r2.x :612-613
318	  const delta = fr32(hi - lo);       // add r1.x, -r2.w, r2 :614
319	  let hue = 0, sat = 0;              // cmp -r1（−Δ²≥0 ⟺ Δ==0）:625/:632 → 灰度像素归零
320	  if (delta !== 0) {
321	    const invD = fr32(1 / delta);    // rcp r1.y :615
322	    // 三扇区色相（HSV 基数 R:+6(≡0,负值归一) G:+2 B:+4；:616-621 通道配对后 r1.w/z/y）
323	    const secR = fr32(fr32(fr32(gf - bf) * invD) + 6); // (g−b)/Δ
324	    const secG = fr32(fr32(fr32(bf - rf) * invD) + 2); // (b−r)/Δ
325	    const secB = fr32(fr32(fr32(rf - gf) * invD) + 4); // (r−g)/Δ
326	    // 达标指示（c−hi ≥ 0 ⟺ c==max，并列取大 hue；:622 add + :626 cmp）→ 三选一（:628-629 max 链）

```

## pass 映射表(反汇编 fxTile.txt 45 pass,`Passes[]` 0 起)

排序槽前导(Pass2-28 公共,fxTile:30-36): `hi=max3`、`lo=min3`、**`midS=min(max(r,g),b)`** — 部分排序中位槽(只排 (r,g) 再与 b 一次 cmp,b 为严格最小时 midS=lo,非真中位)。

| paint | pass | fxTile | 公式(v0=1) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0/31 | 0 | Pass1 :13-22 | 恒等 |
| 1-12 | 1-12 | Pass2-13 | 槽位式:红(hi,lo,lo) 橙(hi,(hi+lo)/2,lo) 黄(hi,**midS**,lo) 青柠((hi+lo)/2,hi,lo) 绿(lo,hi,lo) 蓝绿(lo,hi,(hi+lo)/2) 青(lo,hi,**midS**) 天蓝(lo,(hi+lo)/2,hi) 蓝(lo,lo,hi) 紫((hi+lo)/2,lo,hi) 紫罗兰(hi,lo,**midS**) 粉(hi,lo,(hi+lo)/2) |
| 13-24 | 13-24 | Pass14-25 | 同槽位式但 lo→0.4·lo、(hi+lo)/2→(hi+0.4lo)/2 |
| 25 黑 | 25 | Pass26 :536 | 0.15(hi+lo) |
| 26 白 | 26 | Pass27 :556 | (7hi+3lo)·0.1 ×(2−0.5(hi+lo)) — **提亮曲线非恒等** |
| 27 灰 | 27 | Pass28 :581 | 0.5(hi+lo) |
| 28 棕 | 40 | Pass41 :1321 | (hi, 0.7hi, 0.49hi) — 只吃 hi |
| 29 暗影 | 41 | Pass42 :1339 | 0.025(hi+lo) |
| 30 负相 | 42 | Pass43 :1359 | hi==0 ? c : 1−c(纯黑守卫) |
| 30 负相墙 | 43 | Pass44 :1378 | max(hi==0 ? 2c : 1.5−2c, 0)(GetWallSettings 恒墙 hack) |
| 1-12 特判组 | 28-39 | Pass29-40 | 浅层槽位式 + 逐像素 HSV hue/sat 闭区间四门 + invert(`applyPaintTintSpecial`) |
| — | 44 | Pass45 | 无消费方(hue/sat 调试提取 pass;映射函数最大返回 43) |

实现按 `Math.fround` 逐指令镜像字节码运算序(穷举对比:纯 double 与 f32 序有 ~6% 像素差 1 LSB,f32 序为准);写回 UNORM8 最近偶取整。

## 与旧自造乘色(c·p>>8)的差异 — 哪些漆会变

- **全部非恒等档数值都变**:乘色 → min/max 排序槽调制。例:泥土(151,107,75) 红漆旧=[150,0,0] → 真值=[151,75,75](保留明暗结构,不再归零通道)
- **白漆 26**:旧恒等 → 中灰 128 提亮到 192
- **负相 30**:旧纯黑(0,0,0)→纯白(255,255,255);真值有 hi==0 守卫保持黑(透明边/描线不反白);墙档是陡化两档反转 max(1.5−2c,0),需 `isWall` 位(签名加了可选第 5 参默认 false,调用方零改动)
- **深层 13-24**:旧"同浅层" → 低槽 ×0.4(深层确实有独立强度,pass 13-24 直映无需传参,paint id 已含信息)
- **midS 异常槽**:黄/青/紫罗兰第二满档通道取 min(max(r,g),b) 而非 hi((100,150,200) 黄漆 → (200,150,100),非 (200,200,100)) — 字节码实证的"部分排序"行为

## 未尽项(已登记在 Paint.ts 等价边界注释)

1. **ChunkCache.tintRegion 仍是旧 multiply 合成** — 运行期实际着色路径在该文件(并行会话所有,未动);接线 = 用本函数逐像素(ImageData 循环)替换整矩形 multiply fill
2. **特判组未按 tile type 接线** — `applyPaintTintSpecial` 已实现待消费;需要 TreePaintSystemData 的 per-type 设置表(泥面 0/2/23/109/199/477/492/633、树干 5、棕榈 323、宝石树 584-589、Vanity 595/596/615/616、Ash 634、CullMud 59/60/70)
3. **精度边界**:老 ATI fp24 硬件与 fp32 有 LSB 级差(注释备案);GPU UNORM 平手取整按最近偶兜底,至多 1 LSB
